# FFIC experiment log

Generated by `ffic/exp.py` from `experiments/log.jsonl` (one JSON record per run; nothing is deleted).
All sizes are **ideal code length** in bytes over 20 images (12 Pexels crops 1024x768, 8 Kodak 768x512).
Reference columns: JXL lossless effort 9; FunnelZip (current compressor, Pexels subset of 11 images only).
Verdict compares with the reference experiment **ref_best** (current best, before these experiments): `helps` < -0.20%, `hurts` > +0.20%, otherwise `neutral`. CPU time is the sum over the 20 images.

## PFC goal bias: best mechanism set per image (encoder tries all, signals the choice in 1 byte)

Total 10,182,530 B = -2.85% vs JXL e9, -3.95% vs `ref_best`. Encode cost = sum of all experiments' time (decode = one). Picks: `worlds5_local` x10, `inter_cmatch_excl_inf` x6, `worlds5_localfs_b1` x2, `ct_subgreen` x1, `inter_cmatch_excl` x1


## Details

### `ref_best`

* **Mechanism:** Current best model before this round: nested funnels root->activity->spin->level, RLS predictors with colour taps, parallel-ball blend, error-energy mouth, binary context-mixing residual coder.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Funnels, nesting with inheritance (fractal meta-funnels), parallel balls + global workspace, PE learning, fatigue.
* **Hypothesis:** Reproduces the previous result (10,600,865 B, +1.1% vs JXL e9) byte-for-byte after the rewrite.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb`   code `097151bc78` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:16:42
* **Result:** 10,600,847 B = +1.14% vs JXL e9, +0.00% vs `ref_best`, +1.78% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,555,863 / 5,716,326 / 2,328,658. CPU 79.6 s (wall 20.9 s).

### `field`

* **Mechanism:** Replace the fixed spin/level grid by a continuous FUNNEL FIELD: 7-D descriptor (activity, error energy, wide activity, orientation x strength (2-D), brightness, cross-plane surprise). A pixel lands in the nearest funnel; if outside every mouth a new funnel is created there, inheriting from the nearest. Prediction blends the 3 most resonant funnels.
* **FunnelBrain source:** funnel/field.py find_or_create_funnel (MERGE_RADIUS), funnel/physics.py resonance() (radial x depth pull x fatigue), embed_bytes multi-scale transduction.
* **Hypothesis:** Learned classes fit the image better than a fixed quantisation grid; expect -1 to -3%.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb field=1`   code `02129c03e4` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 09:58:58
* **Result:** 10,617,437 B = +1.30% vs JXL e9, +0.16% vs `ref_best`, +1.85% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,569,499 / 5,716,312 / 2,331,630. CPU 127.1 s (wall 33.1 s).

### `field_m08`

* **Mechanism:** Funnel field with a smaller merge radius (more, narrower funnels).
* **FunnelBrain source:** MERGE_RADIUS (field.py).
* **Hypothesis:** Finer concepts help on large images if inheritance keeps learning cost low.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb field=1 merge=0.08`   code `02129c03e4` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 09:59:31
* **Result:** 10,566,426 B = +0.81% vs JXL e9, -0.32% vs `ref_best`, +1.36% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,543,384 / 5,712,356 / 2,310,689. CPU 124.5 s (wall 33.1 s).

### `field_m18`

* **Mechanism:** Funnel field with a larger merge radius (fewer, wider funnels).
* **FunnelBrain source:** MERGE_RADIUS (field.py).
* **Hypothesis:** Fewer funnels learn faster; may win on small/noisy images.

### `field_k1`

* **Mechanism:** Funnel field, only the landing funnel predicts (no resonance blend of neighbours).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Single landing vs resonance with several mouths.
* **Hypothesis:** Resonance blending of 3 funnels beats a single landing funnel.

### `field_sleep`

* **Mechanism:** Funnel field + SLEEP every 16 rows: FISSION of busy funnels whose landings spread wider than their mouth (split along the widest descriptor axis, both children inherit) and PRUNE of idle young funnels.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Sleep MDL, fission_mixed(), sleep_mdl() in field.py; hypothesis doc section 5.
* **Hypothesis:** Splitting where the data shows two modes sharpens predictors; expect a small gain over field.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb field=1 sleep=16`   code `02129c03e4` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:01:03
* **Result:** 10,600,967 B = +1.14% vs JXL e9, +0.00% vs `ref_best`, +1.66% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,561,634 / 5,715,528 / 2,323,804. CPU 110.4 s (wall 28.9 s).

### `bounce`

* **Mechanism:** BOUNCE PATH context: the funnel ids where the W and N pixels landed plus the current funnel id, hashed, become an extra residual-coder context.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Associations emerge from bounce paths between funnels; no stored edges (hypothesis doc section 2, 4).
* **Hypothesis:** Funnel transitions carry texture continuity that single-pixel contexts miss; small gain.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb bounce=1`   code `02129c03e4` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:01:51
* **Result:** 10,597,311 B = +1.11% vs JXL e9, -0.03% vs `ref_best`, +1.74% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,556,194 / 5,711,950 / 2,329,168. CPU 77.4 s (wall 20.1 s).

### `hippo`

* **Mechanism:** HIPPOCAMPUS: an episodic table keyed by the quantised shape of 7 neighbours (+ G surprise for R/B) stores what followed that shape; recall is an extra ball, an RLS tap and a coder context.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Hippocampus lens stores ball-path episodes; cerebellum predicts the landing from recalled episodes (organs/cerebellum.py).
* **Hypothesis:** Repeated local shapes (texture, JPEG blocks) get predicted from memory; helps Pexels more than Kodak.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb hippo=1`   code `02129c03e4` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:02:52
* **Result:** 10,602,539 B = +1.16% vs JXL e9, +0.02% vs `ref_best`, +1.77% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,554,875 / 5,716,679 / 2,330,981. CPU 90.1 s (wall 23.1 s).

### `lampe`

* **Mechanism:** Online PE reshapes geometry: each funnel's RLS forgetting factor follows its surprise (big error => shorter memory, calm => longer).
* **FunnelBrain source:** reshape_on_surprise() in field.py: surprise widens/deepens the funnel immediately.
* **Hypothesis:** Adapts faster at region boundaries and remembers longer inside regions; small gain.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb lampe=1`   code `02129c03e4` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:03:10
* **Result:** 10,605,365 B = +1.18% vs JXL e9, +0.04% vs `ref_best`, +1.84% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,560,248 / 5,715,913 / 2,329,206. CPU 70.5 s (wall 18.6 s).

### `sal_robust`

* **Mechanism:** Salience damped: surprising pixels get LESS learning weight (robust regression).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Amygdala amplifies energy on salience (inverted here as a control).
* **Hypothesis:** Outliers (edges, noise spikes) corrupt LS predictors; damping them helps.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1`   code `5c1cab3696` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:07:23

### `replay`

* **Mechanism:** SLEEP REPLAY: after each row, the landing funnels re-learn that row once more (the decoder can replay identically).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Offline replay during sleep (hypothesis doc section 2, 5).
* **Hypothesis:** Extra epochs speed learning of young funnels; may overfit (RLS already optimal for one pass).
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb replay=1`   code `02129c03e4` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:05:09
* **Result:** 10,604,254 B = +1.17% vs JXL e9, +0.03% vs `ref_best`, +1.88% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,558,055 / 5,717,242 / 2,328,962. CPU 89.5 s (wall 23.6 s).

### `combo_a`

* **Mechanism:** Field + sleep + bounce + hippocampus + multi-scale together.

### `r_register`

* **Mechanism:** REGISTERS: an R or B funnel is born from G's trained funnel at the same address (spatial weights inherited, colour taps fresh) instead of from its own parent.
* **FunnelBrain source:** MainRegister / SubRegister: nested meta-funnels (parent_meta_id), language register tree (hypothesis doc section 11).
* **Hypothesis:** Each plane starts from scratch today; inheriting G's spatial knowledge saves learning cost on R and B.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 register=1`   code `5c1cab3696` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:09:28
* **Result:** 10,489,498 B = +0.08% vs JXL e9, -1.05% vs `ref_best`, +0.48% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,505,432 / 5,710,389 / 2,273,679. CPU 79.9 s (wall 21.3 s).

### `r_curve`

* **Mechanism:** SPIN AS CURVATURE: an extra ball continuing the local edge trend with its curvature (second difference) from N and W directions.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Wave-ball spin: Magnus term curves the trajectory, never straight (physics.py integrate_step).
* **Hypothesis:** Curved edges and gradients are predicted better than by straight-line balls.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 curve=1`   code `5c1cab3696` (git 1e808d2), run 2026-10-24 10:10:08
* **Result:** 10,511,337 B = +0.29% vs JXL e9, -0.84% vs `ref_best`, +0.63% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,514,597 / 5,712,396 / 2,284,344. CPU 76.5 s (wall 20.0 s).

### `mline`

* **Mechanism:** Material lines: R and B get an extra ball (and RLS tap) from a line R ~ a*G + b fitted on 6 decoded neighbours (ridge 20 toward slope 1).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Colour formation: pixel = light x material x shading; a material under one light moves along a line in colour space as shading changes (dichromatic model).
* **Hypothesis:** Offline test: local material line cuts R prediction error 12% on Kodak (1.82 -> 1.61) but +9% on Pexels; expect a gain on Kodak, neutral on Pexels.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=1`   code `5eea81cd34` (git b73e320), run 2026-10-24 11:04:54
* **Result:** 10,349,431 B = -1.26% vs JXL e9, -2.37% vs `ref_best`, -1.05% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,440,289 / 5,699,365 / 2,209,779. CPU 125.1 s (wall 33.0 s).

### `matf`

* **Mechanism:** Material funnels (mouth 0.02, cap 64, memory ~1000 px, ridge 30) as an extra ball + tap for R and B.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Funnel field in colour space: a material is an attractor; shading (brightness) is the ball's energy, not its position. Colour formation: pixel = light x material x shading.
* **Hypothesis:** Remembering each material's line across the whole image beats refitting it from 6 neighbours; expect a Kodak gain beyond mline.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1`   code `a782f57f3e` (git 0922cfb), run 2026-10-24 11:45:19
* **Result:** 10,340,414 B = -1.34% vs JXL e9, -2.46% vs `ref_best`, -1.09% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,439,106 / 5,699,365 / 2,201,946. CPU 124.5 s (wall 32.6 s).

### `matf_ctx`

* **Mechanism:** Material funnels + material id as a residual-coder context.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Funnel field in colour space: a material is an attractor; shading (brightness) is the ball's energy, not its position. Colour formation: pixel = light x material x shading.
* **Hypothesis:** Different materials have different noise; the coder benefits from knowing which material.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1`   code `a782f57f3e` (git 0922cfb), run 2026-10-24 11:49:19
* **Result:** 10,327,702 B = -1.47% vs JXL e9, -2.58% vs `ref_best`, -1.24% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,432,155 / 5,699,365 / 2,196,183. CPU 127.6 s (wall 33.5 s).

### `matf_lam5`

* **Mechanism:** Material funnels with a weak ridge (slope learned freely).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Funnel field in colour space: a material is an attractor; shading (brightness) is the ball's energy, not its position. Colour formation: pixel = light x material x shading.
* **Hypothesis:** Each material's real slope differs from 1; letting it free helps.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 matlam=5`   code `a782f57f3e` (git 0922cfb), run 2026-10-24 11:49:52

### `gmass`

* **Mechanism:** Gravity mass ball: each funnel keeps a histogram of values that landed in it; the inverse-square-weighted centre of mass within +-8 of the prediction becomes an RLS tap.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Gravity: masses pull with inverse-square force; physics.py GRAVITY / depth pull. Here the masses are pixel values the funnel has landed on before.
* **Hypothesis:** Values that recur (JPEG plateaus, flat sky, saturated highlights) pull predictions; helps Pexels more.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 gmass=1`   code `807c877676` (git 7ea9bc5), run 2026-10-24 12:09:01
* **Result:** 10,325,063 B = -1.49% vs JXL e9, -2.60% vs `ref_best`, -1.25% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,432,715 / 5,695,970 / 2,196,382. CPU 162.0 s (wall 41.6 s).

### `gmass_fast`

* **Mechanism:** Gravity with fast-forgetting masses (histogram halves at 512).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Gravity: masses pull with inverse-square force; physics.py GRAVITY / depth pull. Here the masses are pixel values the funnel has landed on before.
* **Hypothesis:** Local recent values pull more than old ones.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 gmass=1 glim=512`   code `807c877676` (git 7ea9bc5), run 2026-10-24 12:11:00
* **Result:** 10,327,243 B = -1.47% vs JXL e9, -2.58% vs `ref_best`, -1.26% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,432,611 / 5,696,595 / 2,198,038. CPU 146.3 s (wall 38.6 s).

### `ilace`

* **Mechanism:** Coarse-to-fine time: even rows first (own funnels/contexts), then odd rows that also see the row below (3 taps + interpolation ball).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Time: the laws are symmetric (arrow of time chosen by the coder); the early universe is coarse and detail comes later (coarse-to-fine).
* **Hypothesis:** Odd rows gain from two-sided context; even rows lose from distance-2 neighbours. kodim20 quick test: +1.8%.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 ilace=1`   code `807c877676` (git 7ea9bc5), run 2026-10-24 12:11:38
* **Result:** 10,657,746 B = +1.68% vs JXL e9, +0.54% vs `ref_best`, +2.27% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,572,328 / 5,757,128 / 2,328,289. CPU 144.8 s (wall 38.5 s).

### `time_hflip`

* **Mechanism:** Arrow of time: the same coder on every photo mirrored left-right (scan from the right).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Time: the laws are symmetric (arrow of time chosen by the coder); the early universe is coarse and detail comes later (coarse-to-fine).
* **Hypothesis:** Some photos are more predictable in another direction.

### `layers_independent`

* **Mechanism:** Colour layers fully independent: R, G and B planes each coded with no information from the others (no colour taps, no registers; material funnels still read G).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Parallel layers that never interact.
* **Hypothesis:** Much worse: the colour links were the biggest single lever (+18% -> +5% when added).
* **Config:** `ltprewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 register=0`   code `807c877676` (git 853f469), run 2026-10-24 12:47:29
* **Result:** 11,635,149 B = +11.01% vs JXL e9, +9.76% vs `ref_best`, +14.72% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 3,072,891 / 5,699,365 / 2,862,892. CPU 96.0 s (wall 25.7 s).

### `seq_timed`

* **Mechanism:** Current best coder, sequential layers, one image at a time (fair wall-time reference).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Parallel layers that still talk: the three colour layers run at once, each a few rows behind the one it reads (G -> R -> B).
* **Hypothesis:** Same bytes as best_now; wall time = CPU time.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1`   code `75442f9c4e` (git 698173c), run 2026-10-24 12:55:25

### `pipe`

* **Mechanism:** Staggered colour pipeline: G, R, B on three threads; R waits until G is 2 rows ahead, B until R is; R/B funnels are born from G's funnels frozen every 32 rows (R starts after G's first 32 rows).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Parallel layers that still talk: the three colour layers run at once, each a few rows behind the one it reads (G -> R -> B).
* **Hypothesis:** ~2-3x less wall time; bytes within a few hundredths of a percent (registers inherit from a snapshot of G instead of fully trained G).
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 pipe=1 snapk=32`   code `75442f9c4e` (git 698173c), run 2026-10-24 12:56:26
* **Result:** 10,332,754 B = -1.42% vs JXL e9, -2.53% vs `ref_best`, -1.22% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,434,494 / 5,699,365 / 2,198,898. CPU 130.5 s (wall 60.1 s).

### `ct_invert`

* **Mechanism:** Reversible transform: every value inverted (255 - x) before coding.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Colour formation / perception: the same light can be described in other colour coordinates (opponent channels, inverted).
* **Hypothesis:** Near zero: the coder is almost symmetric in brightness, but dark-noise statistics move to the bright end.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1`   code `75442f9c4e` (git 85dc5c2), run 2026-10-24 12:59:20
* **Result:** 10,332,087 B = -1.42% vs JXL e9, -2.54% vs `ref_best`, -1.19% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,434,633 / 5,699,627 / 2,197,827. CPU 130.2 s (wall 34.1 s).

### `ct_subgreen`

* **Mechanism:** Subtract green: store (R-G) mod 256, G, (B-G) mod 256 (WebP's transform).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Colour formation / perception: the same light can be described in other colour coordinates (opponent channels, inverted).
* **Hypothesis:** Worse: the coder already links R and B to G, and the mod-256 wrap breaks smoothness.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1`   code `75442f9c4e` (git 85dc5c2), run 2026-10-24 13:01:07
* **Result:** 11,009,600 B = +5.04% vs JXL e9, +3.86% vs `ref_best`, +4.46% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,689,544 / 5,699,365 / 2,620,691. CPU 141.0 s (wall 37.0 s).

### `inter`

* **Mechanism:** Pixel-by-pixel order: G, R, B of one pixel are coded before the next pixel (three threads hand over per pixel). G gains 4 colour taps: R-G and B-G of the W and N neighbours.
* **FunnelBrain source:** See the colour as one thing (perception binds R, G, B into one colour) instead of three separate layers.
* **Hypothesis:** Small gain on G (it now sees the neighbours' full colour), R/B unchanged; similar wall time to pipe.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 inter=1 snapk=32`   code `d532223e63` (git 2ea657c), run 2026-10-24 13:16:14
* **Result:** 10,362,565 B = -1.13% vs JXL e9, -2.25% vs `ref_best`, -0.98% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,440,328 / 5,711,660 / 2,210,577. CPU 521.6 s (wall 176.6 s).

### `inter_cmatch`

* **Mechanism:** Whole-colour match: before coding a pixel, one yes/no 'is the whole colour equal to W, N, NW or NE?' (+ which one), context-mixed from neighbours' match flags, edge state and how close G's prediction is to a candidate. On a match the R, G, B layers cost nothing.
* **FunnelBrain source:** See the colour as one thing (perception binds R, G, B into one colour) instead of three separate layers.
* **Hypothesis:** Helps on flat / posterised areas (31% of pixels repeat a neighbour's colour); may lose where the per-layer coder already codes those pixels cheaply.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 inter=1 cmatch=1 snapk=32`   code `d532223e63` (git 2ea657c), run 2026-10-24 13:19:16
* **Result:** 10,579,434 B = +0.94% vs JXL e9, -0.20% vs `ref_best`, +2.63% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,262,160 / 6,283,579 / 2,033,692. CPU 538.0 s (wall 181.8 s).

### `inter_cmatch_gated`

* **Mechanism:** Whole-colour match, but the question is only asked where the model already expects a match (p >= 0.55, computable by the decoder); its statistics still learn from every pixel.
* **FunnelBrain source:** See the colour as one thing (perception binds R, G, B into one colour) instead of three separate layers.
* **Hypothesis:** Removes the flag overhead on smooth photos where matches are rare or already cheap. Sweep on 3 images: higher threshold helps Pexels, hurts Kodak.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 inter=1 cmatch=2 mthr=0.55 snapk=32`   code `5706e9a088` (git 2ea657c), run 2026-10-24 13:25:17
* **Result:** 10,505,799 B = +0.23% vs JXL e9, -0.90% vs `ref_best`, +0.94% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,403,216 / 5,925,329 / 2,177,257. CPU 522.8 s (wall 177.0 s).

### `inter_cmatch_excl`

* **Mechanism:** Whole-colour match + 'not a match' exclusion: after a 'no', if this pixel's G and R equal a candidate's, B cannot be that candidate's B, so B's probability is renormalised without those values (ideal cost - log2(1 - P(excluded))).
* **FunnelBrain source:** See the colour as one thing (perception binds R, G, B into one colour) instead of three separate layers.
* **Hypothesis:** Recovers part of the flag's cost on 'no' pixels; only fires when G and R match a candidate (rare), so a small gain.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 inter=1 cmatch=1 mexcl=1 snapk=32`   code `edb83f9fb2` (git 6ce4d89), run 2026-10-24 13:34:31
* **Result:** 10,555,344 B = +0.71% vs JXL e9, -0.43% vs `ref_best`, +2.46% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,262,160 / 6,283,579 / 2,009,602. CPU 605.2 s (wall 203.8 s).

### `inter_cmatch_excl_inf`

* **Mechanism:** As inter_cmatch_excl, and the match question also hears the G layer's own probability for the candidates' G values (extra counter context + mixer input).
* **FunnelBrain source:** See the colour as one thing (perception binds R, G, B into one colour) instead of three separate layers.
* **Hypothesis:** The flag knows when the layered coder is already sure, so it wastes fewer bits on smooth photos.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 inter=1 cmatch=1 mexcl=1 minf=1 snapk=32`   code `edb83f9fb2` (git 6ce4d89), run 2026-10-24 13:38:34

### `wave_only_T8`

* **Mechanism:** A. Waves as the default: the wave tree (split where misfit RMS > 8, regions >= 16 px) is the ONLY guess; the coder stores the tree plus the leftover (context-mixing coder, no funnels).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Waves that split like funnels: one horizontal + one vertical wave per region, a region splits in four where its two waves do not fit.
* **Hypothesis:** Much worse than guessing from neighbours: the tree cannot follow texture and noise, and fine trees cost a lot to store.
* **Config:** `evb wonly=1 wmodel={data}/wave_T8/{name}.wave`   code `4f7f43b49b` (git 1e12622), run 2026-10-25 06:32:57
* **Result:** 20,150,894 B = +92.25% vs JXL e9, +90.09% vs `ref_best`, +104.75% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 6,676,322 / 6,622,026 / 6,652,436. CPU 51.6 s (wall 14.1 s).

### `wave_team_T16`

* **Mechanism:** C. Current best coder + the coarse wave tree (misfit > 16, regions >= 32 px) as an extra guesser and tap for every colour layer; tree bytes added.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Waves that split like funnels: one horizontal + one vertical wave per region, a region splits in four where its two waves do not fit.
* **Hypothesis:** Large-scale shape is already cheap for the funnels; small gain at best, tree is cheap.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 wmodel={data}/wave_T16/{name}.wave`   code `4f7f43b49b` (git 1e12622), run 2026-10-25 06:33:51

### `worlds2_bayes`

* **Mechanism:** Parallel worlds: 2 complete coders (best, old) run side by side; each pixel is coded with their weighted mixture (bayes: weights = cumulative evidence).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Many-worlds / Born rule: every branch exists with a weight; evidence updates the weights; decoherence = branches stop interfering locally.
* **Hypothesis:** Worlds that are right in different places add up; the mixture beats the best single world by a margin that grows with diversity.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 | ltpcrewb   mix=bayes alpha=0.002 beta=1.0`   code `9995f04ae3` (git 7ea9bc5), run 2026-10-24 12:05:40
* **Result:** 10,327,478 B = -1.47% vs JXL e9, -2.58% vs `ref_best`, -1.25% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 0 / 0 / 0. CPU 211.6 s (wall 0 s).

### `worlds5_bayes`

* **Mechanism:** Parallel worlds: 5 complete coders (best, old, fastforget, nofield, salonly) run side by side; each pixel is coded with their weighted mixture (bayes: weights = cumulative evidence).
* **FunnelBrain source:** Many-worlds / Born rule: every branch exists with a weight; evidence updates the weights; decoherence = branches stop interfering locally.
* **Hypothesis:** Worlds that are right in different places add up; the mixture beats the best single world by a margin that grows with diversity.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 | ltpcrewb | ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 lam=0.99 | ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1 | ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3   mix=bayes alpha=0.002 beta=0.5`   code `9995f04ae3` (git 7ea9bc5), run 2026-10-24 12:06:07

### `strips2`

* **Mechanism:** Spatial layers in parallel: each photo cut into 2 horizontal strips, each coded independently with the best single coder on its own core.
* **FunnelBrain source:** Parallel worlds read as parallel layers: independent regions processed at once, combined afterwards.
* **Hypothesis:** About 2x less wall time; size grows because each strip relearns from zero.
* **Config:** `ltpcrewb sal=-1 salp=3 salmax=8 salbias=2 register=1 grav=0.5 field=1 merge=0.08 kres=1 sleep=16 fmax=128 ms=1 bounce=1 salall=2 apm=1 mix2=1 mline=2 matf=1 mctx=1   strips=2`   code `807c877676` (git 853f469), run 2026-10-24 12:48:04
* **Result:** 10,377,104 B = -0.99% vs JXL e9, -2.11% vs `ref_best`, -0.82% vs FunnelZip (Pexels). Planes R/G/B = 2,448,477 / 5,714,348 / 2,214,113. CPU 68.0 s (wall 0 s).
